### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

One mole of an ideal monatomic gas undergoes an adiabatic expansion in which its volume becomes eight times its initial value. If the initial temperature of the gas is <math> <mrow> <mn>100</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> and the universal gas constant <math><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8.0</mn><mi>&#160;J</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>mo</mn><msup><mrow><mn>l</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mn>&#160;K</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>, then how much is the decrease in its internal energy (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>) ?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **900**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>V</mi><mi>i</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>V</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>V</mi><mi>f</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>V</mi></math>

For adiabatic process <math><mo>{</mo><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mn>5/</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math> for monoatomic gas<math><mo>}</mo></math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>T</mi> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>&#8901;</mo><msubsup> <mi>V</mi> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <mi>&#947;</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo><msub> <mi>T</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>&#8901;</mo><msubsup> <mi>V</mi> <mn>2</mn> <mrow> <mi>&#947;</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>V</mi></mfenced><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></msup><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi>V</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></msup></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>K</mn></math>

Loss in internal energy <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><msub><mi>C</mi><mi>v</mi></msub><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mn>100</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>75</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>900</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

</details>

### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Two ideal monoatomic and diatomic gases are mixed with one another to form an ideal gas mixture. The equation of the adiabatic process of the mixture is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>PV</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></msup><mo>=</mo></math> constant, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>11</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac></math> If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> are the number of moles of the monoatomic and diatomic gases in the mixture respectively, find the ratio <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Two cylinders <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> fitted with pistons contain equal amounts of an ideal diatomic gas at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>300</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>. The piston of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is free to move, while that of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is held fixed. The same amount of heat is given to the gas in each cylinder. If the rise in temperature of the gas in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>30</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>, then the rise in temperature of the gas in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo> </mo></math>is.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>30</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>18</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>50</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mn>2</mn><mo>K</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mn>2</mn><mo>K</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is free to move; therefore, heat will be supplied at constant pressure.

      <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi>Q</mi><mtext>A</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><msub><msub><mi>C</mi><mtext>P</mtext></msub><mtext>A</mtext></msub><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi>T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></msub></math>            ...(I)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is held fixed, therefore, heat will be supplied at constant volume.

           <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi>Q</mi><mtext>B</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><msub><msub><mi>C</mi><mtext>V</mtext></msub><mtext>B</mtext></msub><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi>T</mi><mi>B</mi></msub></math>            ...(ii)

But      <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi>Q</mi><mtext>A</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi>Q</mi><mtext>B</mtext></msub></math>

     <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">nC</mtext><mtext>P</mtext></msub><mtext>A</mtext></msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">&#8710;</mtext><msub><mi>T</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">nC</mtext><mtext>v</mtext></msub><mtext>B</mtext></msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">&#8710;</mtext><msub><mi>T</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></msub></math>

          <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi>T</mi><mtext>B</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mfrac><msub><mtext>C</mtext><mtext>P</mtext></msub><msub><mtext>C</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi>T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></msub></math>

          <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi>T</mi><mtext>A</mtext></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>    (<math> <mrow> <mi>&#947;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>1.4</mtext>&#160;</mrow></math>(diatomic) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>&#916;</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

          <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>1.4</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>K</mo> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

    <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi>T</mi><mtext>B</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mn>2</mn><mo>K</mo></math>

</details>

---

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An ideal diatomic gas undergoes a thermodynamic process as shown in the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>V</mi></math> diagram. The process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AB</mi></math> is isochoric while the process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>BC</mi></math> is isothermal. The total heat given to the gas in the process is nearly (use <math><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>0.7</mn></math> )

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image11-1fc341aa-8923-412e-aa46-0e7db358b7b0.jpg" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2.5</mn><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1.4</mn><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3.9</mn><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1.1 </mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>3.9</mn><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>∆</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">W</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>⇒</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">W</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>∆</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">R</mi><mo>∆</mo><mi mathvariant="italic">T</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>∆</mo><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>∆</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>∆</mo><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇒</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2.5</mn><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image12-fcaadcf6-9e43-46dd-8a52-55ae1da2e664.jpg" />

Process BC

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>∆</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">W</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>⇒</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1.4</mn><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">e</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">t</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3.9</mn><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

</details>

---

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Initially, the air in an open vessel is maintained at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>60</mn><mo> </mo><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>. Later on, the vessel is heated to a temperature <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math>, so that one-fourth mass of air escapes from the vessel. If the volume of the vessel remains constant during heating, the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>80</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mo>&#xB0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>444</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mo>&#xB0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>333</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mo>&#xB0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>171</mn><mo> </mo><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>171</mn><mo> </mo><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Since it is an open vessel of constant volume, the pressure and volume of the gas remain constant but the number of moles and the temperature changes.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mi>V</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>R</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>R</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac bevelled="true"><mn>3</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mstyle></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>3</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>60</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>273</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>444</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>≡</mo><mn>171</mn><mo> </mo><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The temperature of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math> moles of a gas at constant volume is changed from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn><mo> </mo><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>120</mn><mo> </mo><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>. The change in internal energy is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>80</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>. The total heat capacity of the gas at constant volume (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>) is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Number of moles <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>mol</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>120</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>80</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math></p><p>Rise in temperature <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>120</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>s</mi><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>T</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>80</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>s</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>20</mn></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> is the molar mass</p><p>The heat capacity is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>s</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> moles of an ideal gas undergoes a process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>B</mi></math> as shown in the diagram. The maximum temperature of the gas during the process is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0029664_Question.jpg" style="width: 180px; height: 169px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">nR</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">nR</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">nR</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">nR</mtext></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">nR</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since the P-V graph of the process is a straight line and two points (V<sub>0</sub>, 2P<sub>0</sub>) and (2V<sub>0</sub>, P<sub>0</sub>) are known, its equation will be

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">P</mtext><mo>-</mo><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴        </mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">P</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext></mrow><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></math>

According to the equation for an ideal gas,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">T</mtext><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">pV</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">nR</mtext></mfrac></math>

  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext></mrow><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">nR</mtext></mfrac></math>

  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext><mo>-</mo><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">nR</mtext><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>                ...(i)

For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math> to maximum, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">dT</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">dV</mtext></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

            <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

or   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>                ...(ii)

Putting this value in Eq. (i), we get

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">T</mtext><mtext>max</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>-</mo><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><msubsup><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">nR</mtext><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">nR</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

One mole of oxygen gas is made to undergo a process in which its molar heat capacity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> depends on its absolute temperature <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math> as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">C</mtext><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mtext mathvariant="italic">T</mtext></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is a positive constant. Work done by the gas when heated from an initial temperature <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> to a final temperature <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>&#x3B1;</mi><msubsup><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#x3B1;</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mi>α</mi> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** none of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mi>α</mi> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mi>v</mi></msub><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>W</mi><mo>'</mo></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>W</mi><mo>'</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>W</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>     (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>W</mi><mo>'</mo></math> is the work done by the gas per unit mole per unit rise in temperature)
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>W</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mi>v</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>R</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#916;</mo><mi>W</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8747;&#160;</mo><msup><mi>W</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></munderover><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>T</mi></math>
        <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Figure shows the variation of the internal energy <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>U</mi></math> with the density <math><mi>ρ</mi></math> of an ideal monoatomic gas for a thermodynamic process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AB</mi></math>. Process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AB</mi></math> is a part of a rectangular hyperbola. Find the work done (in joule) by gas in process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AB</mi></math>.


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image7-ee0d7435-c2b9-4703-a198-5777a838d4e3.jpg" style="max-width:"100%"; object-fit:contain" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image8-959f520c-de99-4620-b142-d7ea67aea52a.png" style="max-width:"100%"; object-fit:contain" />

Suppose final internal energy is <math><mi>x</mi></math>

Process AB is isobaric process

<math><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>&#961;</mi></mrow></msub></math> = constant

<math><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

In process AB

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>n</mi><mi>f</mi><mi>R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

n = number of mole

f = degree of freedom

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>T</mi></math> = Change in temperature

<math><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>n</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>R</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>T</mi></math>

<math><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>T</mi></math>

Work done <math><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>V</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi>P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>V</mi></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>T</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The temperature of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> moles of an ideal gas is increased from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>T</mi></math> through a process for which pressure <math> <mrow> <mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><msup> <mi>T</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math> is a constant. The work done by the gas is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn></mn><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

According to ideal gas equation

          <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mi>P</mi><mi>V</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>or</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>V</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>P</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∵</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>P</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>a</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> </mfrac> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>Given</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mo>…</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>i</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∵</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>V</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>n</mi> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>R</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>a</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒    </mo> <mtext mathvariant="italic">dV</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>2nRT </mo></mrow> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">a</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext mathvariant="italic">dT</mtext> </mrow> </math>

Work done by the gas, <math> <mrow> <mtext>dW = PdV</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>or  </mtext> <mtext mathvariant="italic">W</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> </munderover> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>a</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>n</mi> <mi></mi><mi>R</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>a</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>d</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mo>…</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>Using (i) and (ii)</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

         <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <msubsup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi></mi><mi>n</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mi></mi><mi>R</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mi></mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>6</mn> <mi>n</mi> <mi></mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

During an adiabatic process, the pressure of a gas is found to be proportional to the cube of its absolute temperature. The value of ratio <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi>C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><msub><mi>C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></msub></mfrac></math>  for the gas is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>In an adiabatic process, <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext mathvariant="italic">T</mtext> <mi>&#947;</mi> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>constant</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <msup> <mtext mathvariant="italic">P</mtext> <mrow> <mi>&#947;</mi> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
or  <math>&#160; <mrow> <msup> <mtext mathvariant="italic">T</mtext> <mrow> <mi>&#947;</mi> <mo>/</mo> <mi>&#947;</mi> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>constant</mtext> </mrow></mfenced><mtext mathvariant="italic">&#160;P</mtext> </mrow> </math>

Given <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext mathvariant="italic">T</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>constant</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext mathvariant="italic">&#160;P</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8756;</mo> </mrow> </math>  <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>&#947;</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>&#947;</mi> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>&#8658;</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mi>&#947;</mi> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mi>&#947;</mi> </mrow> </math>

or  <math>&#160; <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>&#947;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>&#8658;</mo> <mi>&#947;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>/</mo> <mn>2</mn></mrow></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><msub><mi>C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

During an adiabatic process, the pressure of an ideal gas is found to be proportional to the cube of its absolute temperature. The ratio <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></msub></mfrac></math> for the gas is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given <math> <mrow> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>&prop;</mo> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>. But for adiabatic process <math> <mrow> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>&prop;</mo> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mi>&gamma;</mi> <mo>/</mo> <mi>&gamma;</mi> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>. So,

      <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>&gamma;</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>&gamma;</mi> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>&rArr;</mo> <mi>&gamma;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>&rArr;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

There is a sample of diatomic gas in a container (whose volume is constant). The temperature of this gas was increased greatly so some molecules fell into atoms (dissociated). The pressure of the gas increased by a factor of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn></math> and the internal energy of the gas increased to a value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn><mo> </mo><mi>times</mi></math> the original internal energy. By what factor did the temperature of the gas (measured in Kelvin) increase?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p> <math><semantics><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>V</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow></semantics></math> ; <math><semantics><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>P</mi><mi>V</mi><mo>=</mo><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">)</mo><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi><mi>′</mi><mo accent="false">⇒</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mi>n</mi></mfrac><mfrac><msup><mi>T</mi><mi>′</mi></msup><mi>T</mi></mfrac></mrow></semantics></math> ... (i)</p><p> <math><semantics><mrow><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">(</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></semantics></math> ; <math><semantics><mrow><mn>4.4</mn><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">(</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi><mi>′</mi><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">(</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi><mi>′</mi><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></semantics></math> </p><p> <math><semantics><mrow><mn>4.4</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">(</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi><msup><mi>T</mi><mi>′</mi></msup><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi><msup><mi>T</mi><mi>′</mi></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">(</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mfrac></mrow></semantics></math> <math><semantics><mrow><mn>4.4</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>6</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mi>n</mi></mfrac><mfrac><msup><mi>T</mi><mi>′</mi></msup><mi>T</mi></mfrac></mrow></semantics></math> ... (ii)</p><p> <math><semantics><mrow><mfrac><mn>6</mn><mn>4.4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>6</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></semantics></math> ; <math><semantics><mrow><mn>30</mn><mspace width="0.167em"></mspace><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn><mspace width="0.167em"></mspace><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>22</mn><mspace width="0.167em"></mspace><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>44</mn><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></semantics></math> ; <math><semantics><mrow><mn>8</mn><mspace width="0.167em"></mspace><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mspace width="0.167em"></mspace><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo accent="false">⇒</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">  </mtext><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></semantics></math> </p><p>Also, <math><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mo accent="false">⇒</mo><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></semantics></math> ; <math><semantics><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">(</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">)</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>T</mi><mi>′</mi></msup><mi>T</mi></mfrac><mo accent="false">⇒</mo><msup><mi>T</mi><mi>′</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>T</mi></mrow></semantics></math> </p>

</details>

---

### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In a given process of an ideal gas, <math>  <mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">dW</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0 </mn> <mtext>and </mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">dQ</mtext> <mo>&lt;</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>. Then for the gas

**Options:**

- **(A)** The temperature will decrease
- **(B)** The volume will increase
- **(C)** The pressure will remain contain
- **(D)** The temperature will increase

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) The temperature will decrease**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>From first law of thermodynamics,

            <math><mrow> <mtext>dQ</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>dU</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>dW</mtext> </mrow> </math>

          <math> <mrow> <mtext>dQ</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>dU</mtext> <mtext>, </mtext> <mtext>if  dW = 0</mtext> </mrow> </math>

Since,      <math> <mrow> <mtext>dQ</mtext> <mo>&lt;</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

Therefore, <math> <mrow> <mtext>dU</mtext> <mo>&lt;</mo> <mn>0 </mn>  <mtext>or </mtext>    <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">U</mtext> <mtext>Final</mtext> </msub> <mo>&lt;</mo> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">U</mtext> <mtext>initial</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

so, the temperature will decrease.

<strong>Note:</strong> Internal energy U of an ideal gas depends only on the temperature of the gas. The internal energy of n moles of an ideal gas is given by nC<sub>v</sub> dT</p>

</details>

---

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Two identical containers <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> having the same volume of an ideal gas at the same temperature have the mass of the gas as <math><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> respectively and <math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>. The gas in each cylinder expands isothermally to double of its volume. If the change in pressure in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>300</mn><mo> </mo><mi>Pa</mi></math>, then the change in pressure in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>200</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Pa</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 130px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>300</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Pa</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 130px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>400</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Pa</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 130px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>500</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Pa</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 130px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>200</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Pa</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 130px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi>V</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>a</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>d</mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>V</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> ………(i)
The process is isothermal <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math><mo>∆</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>∆</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>∆</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>∆</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>∆</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo><mn>300</mn></math>
<math><mo>∆</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>200</mn><mi> </mi><mi>P</mi><mi>a</mi></math>

</details>

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An ideal diatomic gas occupies a volume <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> at a pressure <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>. The gas undergoes a process in which the pressure is proportional to the volume. If at the end of the process the R.M.S. speed of the gas molecules has doubled its initial value, then the heat supplied to the gas, in the given process, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>As <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8733;</mo><mi>V</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><msup><mi>V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>constant</mi></math> </p><p>comparing the above equation with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><msup><mi>V</mi><mi>x</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>constant</mi></math>, we get <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

Also, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mi>V</mi></msub><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>R</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>R</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi></math>

But as RMS speed is doubled therefore temperature becomes four times.
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>&#916;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mi>T</mi><mi>i</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mi>i</mi></msub></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> </p>

</details>

---

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Three moles of an ideal gas undergo a cyclic process shown in figure. The work done by the gas during the process is [Take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>ln</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>693</mn></math>]

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/wenrdgmvqlbstmjwkylmjgyrfpxowpexvqaccfvhfsqrpalsvf.png" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2.36</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>R</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.58</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>R</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1.16</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>R</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>0.58</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>R</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1.16</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>R</mi><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

If <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math> be volume of the gas at <math><mi>C</mi><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mi>W</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>)</mo><mi>l</mi><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>–</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>R</mi><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>l</mi><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>–</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>[</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.693</mn><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>]</mo></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>[</mo><mn>1.386</mn><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>]</mo></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1.16</mn><mi>R</mi><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

</details>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Liquid oxygen at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>50</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> is heated to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>300</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> at a constant pressure of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>atm</mi></math>. The rate of heating is constant. Among the following graphs that represent the variation of temperature with time is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0006080_Ans1_1.jpg" style="max-width:"100%"; object-fit:contain" style="width: 150px; height: 164px;" />
- **(B)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0006080_Ans2_1.jpg" style="max-width:"100%"; object-fit:contain" style="width: 150px; height: 161px;" />
- **(C)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0006080_Ans3_1.jpg" style="max-width:"100%"; object-fit:contain" style="width: 150px; height: 157px;" />
- **(D)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0006080_Ans4_1.jpg" style="max-width:"100%"; object-fit:contain" style="width: 150px; height: 159px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0006080_Ans3_1.jpg" style="max-width:"100%"; object-fit:contain" style="width: 150px; height: 157px;" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

Temperature of liquid oxygen will first increase in the same phase. Then, phase change ( liquid to gas) will take place. During which temperature will remain constant. After that temperature of oxygen in gaseous state will further increase.

</details>

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The molar heat capacity of a monoatomic ideal gas undergoing the process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><msup><mi>V</mi><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>constant</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(D)** Zero

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>R</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For a polytropic process, specific heat of an ideal gas is given by,
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> , </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A diatomic ideal gas is heated at constant volume until the pressure is doubled and again heated at constant pressure until the volume is doubled. The average molar heat capacity for the whole process is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mn>9</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mn>7</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mn>9</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let initial pressure, volume and temperature be P<sub>0</sub>, V<sub>0</sub> and T<sub>0</sub>, respectively, indicated by state A in P-V diagram. The gas is then isochorically taken to state B (2P<sub>0</sub>, V<sub>0</sub>, 2T<sub>0</sub>) and then taken from state B to state C (2P<sub>0</sub>, 2V<sub>0</sub>, 4T<sub>0</sub>) isobarically.


             <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0029739_Ans..jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 237px; height: 195px;" />


Total heat absorbed by 1 mol of gas

          <math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <mtext>Q</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

                  <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext>RT</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext>RT</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>

Total change in temperature from series A to C is <math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>

Therefore,

Molar heat capacity <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <mtext>Q</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext>RT</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>A monoatomic gas of molar mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> is filled into an insulated container which is moving along a horizontal surface with velocity a <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi></math>. If the container is stopped suddenly, then the change in temperature of the gas is</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>If the container is suddenly stopped loss in kinetic energy of the gas is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>n</mi><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> is the number of moles of the gas.</p><p>If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>T</mi></math> is the change in the temperature of the gas, then</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>n</mi><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

One mole of a  certain ideal gas obtains an amount of heat <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>60</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kJ</mi></math> when its temperature is increased by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>Δ</mo><mi>T</mi><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mn>72</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>, keeping its pressure constant. The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi>C</mi><mi>P</mi></msub><msub><mi>C</mi><mi>V</mi></msub></mfrac></math> for the gas is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.60
- **(B)** 1.40
- **(C)** 1.50
- **(D)** 1.30

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 1.60**

**Detailed Solution:**

By the first law of thermodynamics

     <math> <mrow> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>Q</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>U</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> </mrow> </math>

In an isobaric process

      <math> <mrow> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>Q</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> </math>

and     <math> <mrow> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>U</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> </math>      (always)

<math> <mrow> <mo>&there4;   </mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> </mrow> </math>

or   <math> <mrow> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> </math>

or   <math> <mrow> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>R</mtext> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> </math>        <math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>∵</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&there4;   </mo> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>8.3</mtext> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>597.6</mtext> <mo>J</mo> </mrow> </math>

       <math> <mrow> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>Q</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&there4;   </mo> <mtext>1.6</mtext> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&rArr;   </mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>1.6</mtext> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>22.2</mtext> <msup> <mtext> J mol</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>U</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>Q</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>1.6</mtext> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>597.6 J</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>1002.4 J</mtext> </mrow> </math>

But    <math> <mrow> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>U</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&there4;   </mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>1002.4</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>13.9</mtext> <msup> <mtext>J mol</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&there4;   </mo> <mi>&gamma;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>22.2</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>13.9</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>1.60</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>
